# Prompts pour les images

Généré par `php wordpress/tools/make-image-prompts.php` (ne pas modifier à la main).

Style : photo documentaire non mise en scène, finition semi-mate, grain de film, mise au point profonde, sans flou ni brillance. Lumière de jour couvert par défaut, lumière latérale de fin d'après-midi pour les sujets ensoleillés, lumière de fenêtre à l'intérieur ; sol sec par défaut, humide après la pluie ou l'arrosage.

Pour chaque fiche : la **photo principale** (paysage 3:2, par exemple 1536 × 1024 px) et l'**image Pinterest** (portrait 2:3, 1000 × 1500 px). Enregistrez chaque image sous le nom indiqué (`.jpg`, `.png` ou `.webp`), puis importez-les toutes depuis Outils → Natura starter guides → « Import your images » : chaque image se place sur sa fiche grâce à son nom et remplace l'image provisoire.

## Plants

### How to Grow Cherry Tomatoes in Pots

**Photo principale : `cherry-tomato-photo.jpg`**

```
Landscape 3:2 photograph. A cherry tomato plant tied to a bamboo cane in an 8-gallon (30-litre) terracotta pot on a sunny balcony, trusses of small round red and green tomatoes, yellow star-shaped flowers, compound serrated leaves. Unstaged amateur documentary photograph, candid angle, asymmetrical natural framing, no plant labels, no text markers, no neat piles of spilled soil, no posed tools. Late afternoon low-angle warm daylight, strictly side-lit at 45 degrees with zero backlighting, long diagonal cast shadows, no glowing leaf edges. Dry crumbly potting mix with bits of bark and a few small pebbles at the surface, dull non-reflective leaves with fine natural texture and slight insect nibbles. True photographic semi-matte finish, lifted faded shadow blacks, soft rolled-off highlights, low micro-contrast, muted desaturated earth and sage-green tones, no neon greens, zero specular shine, subtle 35mm Kodak Portra 400 film grain, raw unedited field snapshot, 35mm lens at f/8, deep focus, no background blur, no bokeh, no text, no logos, no people's faces.
```

**Image Pinterest : `cherry-tomato-pin.jpg`**

```
Vertical 2:3 photograph. A single cherry tomato plant heavy with ripe red trusses in a large pot against a warm balcony wall, photographed vertically from low down. Unstaged amateur documentary photograph, candid angle, asymmetrical natural framing, no plant labels, no text markers, no neat piles of spilled soil, no posed tools. Overcast cloudy day, flat diffused ambient daylight, soft even shadows, zero backlighting, no glowing leaf edges. Dry crumbly potting mix with bits of bark and a few small pebbles at the surface, dull non-reflective leaves with fine natural texture and slight insect nibbles. True photographic semi-matte finish, lifted faded shadow blacks, soft rolled-off highlights, low micro-contrast, muted desaturated earth and sage-green tones, no neon greens, zero specular shine, subtle 35mm Kodak Portra 400 film grain, raw unedited field snapshot, 35mm lens at f/8, deep focus, no background blur, no bokeh, no text, no logos, no people's faces.
```
